This session is being continued from a previous conversation that ran out of context. The summary below covers the earlier portion of the conversation.

Summary:
1. Primary Request and Intent:
   The user initiated Research Reviewer Loop #9 for the `recursive_gen_depth_est` project. The role is an independent scientific auditor who verifies experiment conclusions, checks data integrity, and reports findings to the Director. The primary task this session was:
   - Confirm handoff and hard constraints from Loop #8
   - Verify whether 4 previously flagged warnings (bib years, forward selection counts, SHAP ρ, literature authors) were fixed
   - Monitor the running experiment `downstream_curation_poc_001` (plan_013: 8 conditions × 5 seeds Pythia-410M LoRA finetune curation PoC)
   - Once complete, audit all PPL results, compute statistical tests, and submit findings to Director
   - All output must be in Chinese, via `reviewer_post_to_director`

2. Key Technical Concepts:
   - **Recursive Generation Depth Estimation**: Predicting how many model→data→model cycles text has undergone (d0-d3)
   - **Curation PoC (plan_013)**: Using RF depth predictions to filter training data, then evaluating downstream Pythia-410M LoRA finetune perplexity on WikiText-103
   - **8 experimental conditions**: unfiltered (10k), rf_strict/moderate/soft (2496/4940/4422), oracle_strict/moderate (2500/5000), random_strict/moderate (2496/4940)
   - **Paired t-test (ttest_rel)** with Cohen's d for statistical comparison
   - **LoRA**: r=16, alpha=32, dropout=0.05, target_modules=["query_key_value"], Pythia-410M (406M params, 1.57M trainable)
   - **Training config**: 3 epochs, batch_size=8, gradient_accumulation=4 (effective batch=32), lr=2e-4
   - **Eval**: WikiText-103 test set PPL, stride=512, max_length=1024
   - **Paper target**: EMNLP 2026 ARR May 25 (DDL ~10 days), Claim 3 is "Discussion practical implication"
   - **Depth Degradation Atlas + EDD**: Primary contribution (Claims 1+2)
   - **Server**: gpu-6000pro-2, 2× NVIDIA RTX PRO 6000 Blackwell (96GB each), project uses both cuda:0 and cuda:1

3. Files and Code Sections:
   - **Remote: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/results/curation_poc/`** — All 40 ppl_result.json files individually read and verified. Each contains: condition, seed, perplexity, train_loss, train_time_s, n_samples.
   - **Remote: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/data/curation_poc/data_prep_summary.json`** — Shows depth distribution per condition. Key: rf_strict has 2478/2496 d0, oracle_strict is pure d0 (2500), random_strict is uniform ~625/depth.
   - **Remote: `/root/autodl-tmp/recursive_gen_depth_est/src/curation_poc.py`** — Training script. analyze phase uses `stats.ttest_rel(baseline, vals)` + `cohens_d = delta / pooled_std`. Post-completion command `--phase analyze` registered but NOT auto-triggered.
   - **Local: `docs/paper/references.bib`** — Verified holtzman2020_nucleus year=2020 ✓, basu2021_mirostat year=2021 ✓, shi2025_closer_look (corrected from he2025) ✓, yi2026_escaping_collapse (corrected from feng2025) ✓
